    \paragraph{Our dataset format}\label{our-dataset-format}

An event is a list of strings.

A sequence is a list of events.

A dataset is a list of sequences.

Thus, a dataset is a list of lists of lists of strings.

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

    \section{2- Foundations}\label{foundations}

\subsubsection{Subsequences}\label{subsequences}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}3}]:} \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{l+s+sd}{This is a simple recursive method that checks if subsequence is a subSequence of mainSequence}
        \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{isSubsequence}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mainSequence}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{subSequence}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{subSequenceClone} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{list}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{subSequence}\PY{p}{)} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} clone the sequence, because we will alter it}
            \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{isSubsequenceRecursive}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mainSequence}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{subSequenceClone}\PY{p}{)} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}start recursion}
        
        \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{l+s+sd}{Function for the recursive call of isSubsequence, not intended for external calls}
        \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{isSubsequenceRecursive}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mainSequence}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{subSequenceClone}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{start}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Check if empty: End of recursion, all itemsets have been found}
            \PY{k}{if} \PY{p}{(}\PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{subSequenceClone}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                \PY{k}{return} \PY{k+kc}{True}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} retrieves element of the subsequence and removes is from subsequence }
            \PY{n}{firstElem} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{subSequenceClone}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Search for the first itemset...}
            \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{start}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mainSequence}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                \PY{k}{if} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mainSequence}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{issuperset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{firstElem}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} and recurse}
                    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{isSubsequenceRecursive}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mainSequence}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{subSequenceClone}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o}{+} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{k}{return} \PY{k+kc}{False}
\end{Verbatim}

    \section{3- PrefixSpan algorithm}\label{prefixspan-algorithm}

\subsubsection{Project a sequence}\label{project-a-sequence}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}14}]:} \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Projects a sequence according to a given prefix, as done in PrefixSpan}
         
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Args:}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    sequence: the sequence the projection is built from}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    prefix: the prefix that is searched for in the sequence}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    newEvent: if set to True, the first itemset is ignored}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Returns:}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    If the sequence does not contain the prefix, then None.}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    Otherwise, a new sequence starting from the position of the prefix, including the itemset that includes the prefix}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{projectSequence}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{sequence}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{newEvent}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{n}{result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{None}
             \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{itemset} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{sequence}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                 \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{result} \PY{o+ow}{is} \PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{k}{if} \PY{p}{(}\PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{newEvent}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o+ow}{or} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
                         \PY{k}{if} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{all}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{itemset} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{x} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                             \PY{n}{result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n+nb}{list}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{itemset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}
                 \PY{k}{else}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{n}{result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{copy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{copy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{itemset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{result}
\end{Verbatim}

         \PY{n}{projectSequence}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{seq}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{b}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}

    \subsubsection{Project a dataset}\label{project-a-dataset}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}19}]:} \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Projects a dataset according to a given prefix, as done in PrefixSpan}
         
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Args:}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    dataset: the dataset the projection is built from}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    prefix: the prefix that is searched for in the sequence}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    newEvent: if set to True, the first itemset is ignored}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Returns:}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    A (potentially empty) list of sequences}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{projectDatabase}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{newEvent}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{n}{projectedDB} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
             \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{sequence} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{:}
                 \PY{n}{seqProjected} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{projectSequence}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{sequence}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{newEvent}\PY{p}{)}
                 \PY{k}{if} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{seqProjected} \PY{o+ow}{is} \PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{n}{projectedDB}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{seqProjected}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{projectedDB}
\end{Verbatim}


    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

    \subsubsection{The main algorithm}\label{the-main-algorithm}

    \paragraph{Some more utility
functions:}\label{some-more-utility-functions}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}23}]:} \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Generates a list of all items that are contained in a dataset}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{generateItems}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb}{sorted}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{set} \PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{item} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{sublist1} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{dataset} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{sublist2} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{sublist1} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{item} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{sublist2}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
         
         \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Computes a defaultdict that maps each item in the dataset to its support}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{generateItemSupports}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ignoreFirstEvent}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{n}{result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{defaultdict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{int}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{sequence} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{:}
                 \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{ignoreFirstEvent}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{n}{sequence} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{sequence}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}
                 \PY{n}{cooccurringItems} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
                 \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{itemset} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{sequence}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{all}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{itemset} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{x} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                         \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{item} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{itemset}\PY{p}{:}
                             \PY{k}{if} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{item} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{prefix}\PY{p}{:}
                                 \PY{n}{cooccurringItems}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{add}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{)}
                 \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{item} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{cooccurringItems}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{n}{result} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}
             \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb}{sorted}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{items}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}


    \paragraph{Finally, the algorithm:}\label{finally-the-algorithm}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}24}]:} \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{The PrefixSpan algorithm. Computes the frequent sequences in a seqeunce dataset for a given minFrequency}
         
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Args:}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    dataset: A list of sequences, for which the frequent (sub\PYZhy{})sequences are computed}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    minFrequency: The minimum support that makes a sequence frequent}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{Returns:}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{    A list of tuples (s, c), where s is a frequent sequence, and c is the count for that sequence}
         \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
         \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{prefixSpan}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{minFrequency}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{n}{result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
             \PY{n}{itemCounts} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{generateItemSupports}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{count} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{itemCounts}\PY{p}{:}
                 \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{count} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{minFrequency}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{n}{newPrefix} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}
                     \PY{n}{result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{newPrefix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{count}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
                     \PY{n}{result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{prefixSpanInternal}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{projectDatabase}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{minFrequency}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{newPrefix}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{result}
         
         \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{prefixSpanInternal}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{minFrequency}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{prevPrefixes}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{n}{result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
             
             \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Add a new item to the last element (==same time)}
             
                 
             \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Add a new event to the prefix}
             \PY{n}{itemCountSubsequentEvents} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{generateItemSupports}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{count} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{itemCountSubsequentEvents}\PY{p}{:}
                 \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{count} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{minFrequency}\PY{p}{:}
                     \PY{n}{newPrefix} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{copy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{deepcopy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{prevPrefixes}\PY{p}{)}
                     \PY{n}{newPrefix}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
                     \PY{n}{result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{newPrefix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{count}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
                     \PY{n}{result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{prefixSpanInternal}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{projectDatabase}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{minFrequency}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{newPrefix}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{result}
\end{Verbatim}

    \section{4- Practical work: analyzing YahooFinance
data}\label{practical-work-analyzing-yahoofinance-data}

Now we try to find some sequential patterns in a real world dataset.

First load the dataset.

    Binôme : Yulin Chai, Lucas Pagano

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}153}]:} \PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n+nn}{csv}
          
          \PY{n}{currentLineNumber}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}
          \PY{n}{idSeq}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{1}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}
          \PY{n}{newTransaction}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
          \PY{n}{yahooDataset}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
          
          \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{line} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{csv}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{reader}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{row} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{row} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{open}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{YahooFinance.data}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{if} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{row}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{endswith}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{0}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{delimiter}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}t}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
              \PY{n}{currentLineNumber}\PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}
              \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{currentLineNumber}\PY{o}{==}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
                  \PY{k}{continue}
              \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{line}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{!=} \PY{n}{idSeq}\PY{p}{:}
                  \PY{n}{yahooDataset}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{newTransaction}\PY{p}{)}
                  \PY{n}{idSeq}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{line}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}
                  \PY{n}{newTransaction}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
              \PY{n}{newTransaction}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{line}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}41}]:} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Q1}
         \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}On extrait les fenêtres}
         
         \PY{n}{windows} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
         \PY{n}{max\PYZus{}span} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{4}
         \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{event} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{yahooDataset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
             \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{i}\PY{o}{+}\PY{n}{max\PYZus{}span} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{yahooDataset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:} \PY{k}{break}
             \PY{n}{window} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
             \PY{n}{window}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{event}\PY{p}{)}
             \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{j}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{event2} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{yahooDataset}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                 \PY{n}{window}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{event2}\PY{p}{)}
                 \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{j} \PY{o}{==} \PY{n}{max\PYZus{}span}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:} \PY{k}{break}
             \PY{n}{windows}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{window}\PY{p}{)}
             
         \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{windows}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
         \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{yahooDataset}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}

{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}163}]:} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Q3 : On crée un dictionnaire pour répertorier les informations sur les évènements}
          \PY{n}{dictEvents} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}L est le nombre total de fenêtres vu qu\PYZsq{}on compte par fenêtre}
          \PY{n}{L} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{windows}\PY{p}{)}
          \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{tupleEvent} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{eventsFrequency}\PY{p}{:}
              \PY{n}{eventName} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{join}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{elem}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{elem} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{tupleEvent}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
              
              \PY{n}{dictEvents}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{eventName}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{count}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{tupleEvent}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{support}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{tupleEvent}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{/}\PY{n}{L}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
          
          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Lift}
          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}On ne le fait pas au premier passage car on a besoin que tous les évènements soient rajoutés}
          \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{tupleEvent} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{eventsFrequency}\PY{p}{:}
              \PY{n}{eventName} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{join}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{elem}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{elem} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{tupleEvent}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
              \PY{n}{freqNum} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dictEvents}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{eventName}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{support}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}
              \PY{n}{freqDenum} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{prod}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{dictEvents}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{elem}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{support}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{elem} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{tupleEvent}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
              \PY{n}{dictEvents}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{eventName}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{lift}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{freqNum}\PY{o}{/}\PY{n}{freqDenum}
          
          \PY{n}{dictEvents}
          
              
\end{Verbatim}

{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}168}]:} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Poursuite de l\PYZsq{}analyse}
          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Évènements qui arrivent sur la même entreprise}
          \PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{sameCompany}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{string}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{rules}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
              \PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
          \PY{l+s+sd}{    Fonction qui renvoie vrai si tous les évènements arrivent sur la même entreprise, et faux sinon}
          \PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{rules}\PY{p}{:}
                  \PY{n}{string} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{string}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{replace}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZhy{}\PYZgt{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
              \PY{n}{companies} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{string}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{split}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
              \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{companies}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
                  \PY{n}{withoutVariation} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
                  \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{company} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{companies}\PY{p}{:}
                      \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}on prend ce qu\PYZsq{}il y a avant le =}
                      \PY{n}{withoutVariation}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{company}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{split}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
                  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}return True if all elems are equal, false if not}
                  \PY{n+nb}{bool} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{withoutVariation}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{count}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{withoutVariation}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{==} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{withoutVariation}\PY{p}{)}
              \PY{k}{else}\PY{p}{:} 
                  \PY{n+nb}{bool} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{False}
              \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb}{int}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{bool}\PY{p}{)}
              
          
          
          \PY{n}{episodes\PYZus{}list} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
          \PY{n}{rules\PYZus{}list} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}rules}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
          \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{same}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=}  \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{sameCompany}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{episode}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{episode} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{episodes\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{]}
          \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}rules}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{same}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=}  \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{sameCompany}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{rule}\PY{p}{,} \PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{rule} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{rules\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{]}
          
          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}taille de l\PYZsq{}évènement}
          \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{length}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{episode}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{split}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{episode} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{episodes\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{]}
          \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}rules}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{length}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{rule}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{split}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{rule} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{rules\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{]}

    On remarque que la taille est discriminante pour le lift des épisodes,
et surtout pour les règles.

    Question 6 : Intuitivement, les meilleurs épisodes de taille 2 sont ceux
qui s'appliquent sur la même entreprise. En effet une entreprise a plus
de chances d'influencer son propre état que celui des autres
entreprises. On le vérifie.

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
{\color{incolor}In [{\color{incolor}150}]:} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes\PYZus{}2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{length}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{]}
          \PY{n}{sns}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pairplot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes\PYZus{}2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hue}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{same}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb}{vars}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}episodes\PYZus{}2}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{same}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{count}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{length}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

les données.
